     1	import { DefaultTheme, ThemeProvider } from "@react-navigation/native";
     2	import { Stack, usePathname } from "expo-router";
     3	import { useFonts } from "expo-font";
     4	import * as NavigationBar from "expo-navigation-bar";
     5	import {
     6	  Animated,
     7	  AppState,
     8	  Platform,
     9	  Text,
    10	  TextInput,
    11	  View,
    12	} from "react-native";
    13	import {
    14	  SafeAreaProvider,
    15	  useSafeAreaInsets,
    16	} from "react-native-safe-area-context";
    17	import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
    18	
    19	import AppHeader from "../components/AppHeader";
    20	import MainScreenPushFrame from "../components/MainScreenPushFrame";
    21	import ScreenFade from "../components/ScreenFade";
    22	import StickyCartButton from "../components/StickyCartButton";
    23	import {
    24	  BackgroundHeroStateProvider,
    25	  useBackgroundHeroState,
    26	} from "../utils/backgroundHeroStateContext";
    27	import { HeaderScrollProvider } from "../utils/headerScrollContext";
    28	import {
    29	  HeaderSwipeProvider,
    30	  useHeaderSwipe,
    31	} from "../utils/headerSwipeContext";
    32	import { getTopSafeInset } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    33	import { ShopCartProvider, useShopCart } from "../utils/shopCartContext";
    34	
    35	const navigationTheme = {
    36	  ...DefaultTheme,
    37	  colors: {
    38	    ...DefaultTheme.colors,
    39	    background:
    40	      Platform.OS === "web" ? "transparent" : DefaultTheme.colors.background,
    41	    card: Platform.OS === "web" ? "transparent" : DefaultTheme.colors.card,
    42	  },
    43	};
    44	const androidNavigationBarColor = "#f7b967";
    45	const androidNavigationBarHairlineColor = "rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.28)";
    46	const androidNavigationBarHairlineWidth = 0.375;
    47	const androidNavigationBarButtonStyle = "light";
    48	
    49	function disableAutomaticFontScaling(Component) {
    50	  Component.defaultProps = Component.defaultProps || {};
    51	  Component.defaultProps.allowFontScaling = false;
    52	  Component.defaultProps.maxFontSizeMultiplier = 1;
    53	}
    54	
    55	disableAutomaticFontScaling(Text);
    56	disableAutomaticFontScaling(TextInput);
    57	
    58	async function applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme() {
    59	  if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;
    60	
    61	  try {
    62	    await NavigationBar.setVisibilityAsync("visible");
    63	  } catch {}
    64	
    65	  try {
    66	    await NavigationBar.setButtonStyleAsync(androidNavigationBarButtonStyle);
    67	  } catch {}
    68	}
    69	
    70	function AndroidNavigationBarTint({ pathname }) {
    71	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    72	
    73	  useEffect(() => {
    74	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") return undefined;
    75	
    76	    applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme();
    77	    const restoreTimer = setTimeout(applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme, 250);
    78	    const finalRestoreTimer = setTimeout(applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme, 1000);
    79	    const appStateSubscription = AppState.addEventListener("change", (state) => {
    80	      if (state === "active") {
    81	        applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme();
    82	      }
    83	    });
    84	    let visibilitySubscription;
    85	
    86	    try {
    87	      visibilitySubscription = NavigationBar.addVisibilityListener(
    88	        applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme
    89	      );
    90	    } catch {}
    91	
    92	    return () => {
    93	      clearTimeout(restoreTimer);
    94	      clearTimeout(finalRestoreTimer);
    95	      appStateSubscription.remove();
    96	      visibilitySubscription?.remove();
    97	    };
    98	  }, [pathname]);
    99	
   100	  if (Platform.OS !== "android" || insets.bottom <= 0) {
   101	    return null;
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  return (
   105	    <View
   106	      pointerEvents="none"
   107	      style={{
   108	        position: "absolute",
   109	        left: 0,
   110	        right: 0,
   111	        bottom: 0,
   112	        height: insets.bottom,
   113	        borderTopWidth: androidNavigationBarHairlineWidth,
   114	        borderTopColor: androidNavigationBarHairlineColor,
   115	        backgroundColor: androidNavigationBarColor,
   116	        zIndex: 1000001,
   117	        elevation: 1000001,
   118	      }}
   119	    />
   120	  );
   121	}
   122	
   123	function RootLayoutContent({ headerScrollY }) {
   124	  const pathname = usePathname();
   125	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
   126	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   127	  const { cartOverlayActionRequest, isShopOverlayVisible } = useShopCart();
   128	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   129	  const topSafeInset = getTopSafeInset(insets);
   130	
   131	  const showPersistentHeader = pathname !== "/shop";
   132	
   133	  const useOverlayHeader =
   134	    pathname === "/" ||
   135	    pathname === "/products" ||
   136	    pathname === "/aboutus" ||
   137	    pathname === "/contact";
   138	
   139	  const shouldProtectBackgroundHero =
   140	    useOverlayHeader &&
   141	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive || backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen);
   142	  const backgroundHeroLayerDepth = shouldProtectBackgroundHero ? 100 : 0;
   143	  const screenFadeTopOffset = pathname === "/shop"
   144	    ? 120 + topSafeInset
   145	    : 84 + topSafeInset;
   146	  const shouldShowScreenFade =
   147	    !(pathname === "/shop" && isShopOverlayVisible);
   148	  const shouldShowCartOpeningDim =
   149	    cartOverlayActionRequest.pending && pathname !== "/shop";
   150	
   151	  return (
   152	    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2" }}>
   153	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   154	        <View
   155	          pointerEvents="none"
   156	          style={{
   157	            position: "absolute",
   158	            top: 0,
   159	            left: 0,
   160	            right: 0,
   161	            zIndex: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   162	            elevation: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   163	          }}
   164	        >
   165	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showOnlyHero />
   166	        </View>
   167	      ) : null}
   168	
   169	      <MainScreenPushFrame>
   170	        <Stack
   171	          screenOptions={{
   172	            headerShown: false,
   173	            animation: "none",
   174	            contentStyle: {
   175	              backgroundColor: useOverlayHeader
   176	                ? "transparent"
   177	                : "#FFFCF2",
   178	            },
   179	          }}
   180	        />
   181	      </MainScreenPushFrame>
   182	
   183	      {shouldShowScreenFade ? (
   184	        <ScreenFade
   185	          showTopFade={!useOverlayHeader}
   186	          topOffset={screenFadeTopOffset}
   187	        />
   188	      ) : null}
   189	
   190	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   191	        <View
   192	          pointerEvents="box-none"
   193	          style={{
   194	            position: "absolute",
   195	            top: 0,
   196	            left: 0,
   197	            right: 0,
   198	            zIndex: 1000000,
   199	            elevation: 1000000,
   200	          }}
   201	        >
   202	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showHero={false} />
   203	        </View>
   204	      ) : null}
   205	
   206	      {showPersistentHeader && !useOverlayHeader ? (
   207	        <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} />
   208	      ) : null}
   209	
   210	      {shouldShowCartOpeningDim ? (
   211	        <View
   212	          pointerEvents="none"
   213	          style={{
   214	            position: "absolute",
   215	            top: 0,
   216	            right: 0,
   217	            bottom: 0,
   218	            left: 0,
   219	            backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
   220	            zIndex: 1000001,
   221	            elevation: 1000001,
   222	          }}
   223	        />
   224	      ) : null}
   225	
   226	      <StickyCartButton />
   227	
   228	      <AndroidNavigationBarTint pathname={pathname} />
   229	    </View>
   230	  );
   231	}
   232	
   233	export default function RootLayout() {
   234	  const headerScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   235	
   236	  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
   237	    "Dream Avenue": require("../assets/fonts/dream_avenue/dream_avenue.ttf"),
   238	    "TT Fors": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_regular.ttf"),
   239	    "TT Fors Demibold": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_demibold.ttf"),
   240	    "TT Fors Light": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_light.ttf"),
   241	  });
   242	
   243	  if (!fontsLoaded) {
   244	    return null;
   245	  }
   246	
   247	  return (
   248	    <SafeAreaProvider>
   249	      <ThemeProvider value={navigationTheme}>
   250	        <HeaderScrollProvider scrollY={headerScrollY}>
   251	          <BackgroundHeroStateProvider sourceScrollY={headerScrollY}>
   252	            <HeaderSwipeProvider>
   253	              <ShopCartProvider>
   254	                <RootLayoutContent headerScrollY={headerScrollY} />
   255	              </ShopCartProvider>
   256	            </HeaderSwipeProvider>
   257	          </BackgroundHeroStateProvider>
   258	        </HeaderScrollProvider>
   259	      </ThemeProvider>
   260	    </SafeAreaProvider>
   261	  );
   262	}

FILE: mob/app/index.js

     1	import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     2	import {
     3	  Animated,
     4	  Image,
     5	  StyleSheet,
     6	  Text,
     7	  View,
     8	  useWindowDimensions,
     9	} from "react-native";
    10	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
    11	
    12	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
    13	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
    14	import PageDivider from "../components/PageDivider";
    15	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
    16	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
    17	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    18	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    19	
    20	const passionImageBlendScrollDistance = 720;
    21	const passionImageBlendFeatherHeight = 170;
    22	const passionImageBlendOverlayColor = "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.92)";
    23	const featureImageAspectRatio = 1280 / 853;
    24	const homeFeatureMagnifiedScale = 1.08;
    25	const homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio = 0.5;
    26	
    27	function isMatchingLayout(currentLayout, nextLayout) {
    28	  return (
    29	    currentLayout &&
    30	    Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
    31	    Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
    32	  );
    33	}
    34	
    35	export default function HomeScreen() {
    36	  const [imageLayouts, setImageLayouts] = useState({});
    37	  const [paragraphLayouts, setParagraphLayouts] = useState({});
    38	  const {
    39	    compactTopLayout,
    40	    initialContentOffset,
    41	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    42	    scrollHandlers,
    43	    scrollY,
    44	  } =
    45	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    46	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    47	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    48	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
    49	  const croppedImageHeight = croppedImageWidth * featureImageAspectRatio;
    50	  const passionImageBlendTranslateY = scrollY.interpolate({
    51	    inputRange: [0, passionImageBlendScrollDistance],
    52	    outputRange: [-passionImageBlendFeatherHeight, croppedImageHeight],
    53	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    54	  });
    55	  const handleParagraphLayout = useCallback(
    56	    (featureName) =>
    57	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    58	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    59	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    60	
    61	        setParagraphLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    62	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    63	            return currentLayouts;
    64	          }
    65	
    66	          return {
    67	            ...currentLayouts,
    68	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    69	          };
    70	        });
    71	      },
    72	    []
    73	  );
    74	  const handleImageLayout = useCallback(
    75	    (featureName) =>
    76	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    77	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    78	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    79	
    80	        setImageLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    81	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    82	            return currentLayouts;
    83	          }
    84	
    85	          return {
    86	            ...currentLayouts,
    87	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    88	          };
    89	        });
    90	      },
    91	    []
    92	  );
    93	
    94	  return (
    95	    <View style={sharedStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    96	      <Animated.ScrollView
    97	        style={sharedStyles.scroll}
    98	        contentContainerStyle={[
    99	          sharedStyles.scrollContent,
   100	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   101	        ]}
   102	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   103	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   104	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   105	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   106	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   107	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   108	        {...scrollHandlers}
   109	      >
   110	        <View style={sharedStyles.main}>
   111	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   112	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   113	            pageTitleStyle={[sharedStyles.pageTitle, homeStyles.pageTitle]}
   114	            scrollY={scrollY}
   115	          />
   116	
   117	          <CenterMagnifyView
   118	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.passion}
   119	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.passion}
   120	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   121	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   122	            scrollY={scrollY}
   123	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   124	          >
   125	            <View
   126	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("passion")}
   127	              style={[
   128	                sharedStyles.directionalImageBlendWrap,
   129	                { width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight },
   130	              ]}
   131	            >
   132	              <Image
   133	                pointerEvents="none"
   134	                source={require("../passion111_mos9_bright_italian_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   135	                style={[
   136	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   137	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   138	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   139	                ]}
   140	                resizeMode="contain"
   141	              />
   142	              <Animated.View
   143	                pointerEvents="none"
   144	                style={[
   145	                  sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendPanel,
   146	                  {
   147	                    height:
   148	                      croppedImageHeight + passionImageBlendFeatherHeight,
   149	                    transform: [{ translateY: passionImageBlendTranslateY }],
   150	                  },
   151	                ]}
   152	              >
   153	                <LinearGradient
   154	                  colors={[
   155	                    "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
   156	                    passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   157	                  ]}
   158	                  locations={[0, 1]}
   159	                  style={sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendFeather}
   160	                />
   161	                <View
   162	                  style={[
   163	                    sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendSolid,
   164	                    {
   165	                      height: croppedImageHeight,
   166	                      backgroundColor: passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   167	                    },
   168	                  ]}
   169	                />
   170	              </Animated.View>
   171	            </View>
   172	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   173	              Passion
   174	            </Text>
   175	            <Text
   176	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("passion")}
   177	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   178	            >
   179	              Every Alla Vostra board is prepared with care, precision, and a
   180	              love for the grazing experience.
   181	            </Text>
   182	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   183	
   184	          <PageDivider />
   185	
   186	          <CenterMagnifyView
   187	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.taste}
   188	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.taste}
   189	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   190	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   191	            scrollY={scrollY}
   192	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   193	          >
   194	            <View
   195	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("taste")}
   196	              style={{ width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight }}
   197	            >
   198	              <Image
   199	                pointerEvents="none"
   200	                source={require("../taste111_mos9_bright_soft_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   201	                style={[
   202	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   203	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   204	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   205	                ]}
   206	                resizeMode="contain"
   207	              />
   208	            </View>
   209	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   210	              Taste
   211	            </Text>
   212	            <Text
   213	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("taste")}
   214	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   215	            >
   216	              Our boards are built around layered flavor: cheeses, meats,
   217	              fruits, sweets, spreads, and accoutrements.
   218	            </Text>
   219	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   220	
   221	          <PageDivider />
   222	
   223	          <CenterMagnifyView
   224	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.convenience}
   225	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.convenience}
   226	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   227	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   228	            scrollY={scrollY}
   229	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   230	          >
   231	            <View
   232	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("convenience")}
   233	              style={{ width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight }}
   234	            >
   235	              <Image
   236	                pointerEvents="none"
   237	                source={require("../convenience111_mos9_bright_soft_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   238	                style={[
   239	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   240	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   241	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   242	                ]}
   243	                resizeMode="contain"
   244	              />
   245	            </View>
   246	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   247	              Convenience
   248	            </Text>
   249	            <Text
   250	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("convenience")}
   251	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   252	            >
   253	              Alla Vostra brings the board to you for celebrations, family
   254	              gatherings, and larger events.
   255	            </Text>
   256	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   257	        </View>
   258	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   259	    </View>
   260	  );
   261	}
   262	
   263	const homeStyles = StyleSheet.create({
   264	  pageTitle: {
   265	    lineHeight: 64.5,
   266	  },
   267	
   268	  featureTitle: {
   269	    lineHeight: 57,
   270	  },
   271	
   272	  featureText: {
   273	    lineHeight: 65.25,
   274	  },
   275	});

    93	function ProductSection({ product, croppedImageWidth }) {
    94	  return (
    95	    <View style={productsStyles.productCard}>
    96	      <View style={{ width: croppedImageWidth }}>
    97	        <View style={productsStyles.productImageWrap}>
    98	          <Image
    99	            pointerEvents="none"
   100	            source={product.image}
   101	            style={[
   102	              productsStyles.productImage,
   103	              { height: croppedImageWidth },
   104	            ]}
   105	            resizeMode="contain"
   106	            accessibilityLabel={product.alt}
   107	          />
   108	        </View>
   109	      </View>
   110	
   111	      <Text style={productsStyles.productTitle}>{product.title}</Text>
   112	      <Text style={productsStyles.productDescription}>{product.serving}</Text>
   113	
   114	      <View style={productsStyles.productDetails}>
   115	        {product.includes.map((section) => (
   116	          <View key={section.title} style={productsStyles.includeSection}>
   117	            <View style={productsStyles.includeHeaderRow}>
   118	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeTitle}>{section.title}</Text>
   119	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeAmount}>{section.amount}</Text>
   120	            </View>
   121	
   122	            {section.items.map((item) => (
   123	              <View key={item} style={productsStyles.includeItemRow}>
   124	                <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleBorder}>
   125	                  <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleOuter} />
   126	                  <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangle} />
   127	                </View>
   128	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeItem}>{item}</Text>
   129	              </View>
   130	            ))}
   131	
   132	            {section.note ? (
   133	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeNote}>{section.note}</Text>
   134	            ) : null}
   135	          </View>
   136	        ))}
   137	      </View>
   138	    </View>
   139	  );
   140	}
   141	
   142	export default function ProductsScreen() {
   143	  const {
   144	    compactTopLayout,
   145	    initialContentOffset,
   146	    scrollContentInsetStyle,

   148	    scrollY,
   149	  } =

   151	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
   152	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   153	  const croppedImageWidth = windowWidth * 1.05;
   154	
   155	  return (
   156	    <View style={productsStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   157	      <Animated.ScrollView
   158	        style={productsStyles.scroll}
   159	        contentContainerStyle={[
   160	          productsStyles.scrollContent,
   161	          scrollContentInsetStyle,

   175	            scrollY={scrollY}
   176	          />
   177	
   178	          {products.map((product, index) => (
   179	            <Fragment key={product.title}>
   180	              <CenterMagnifyView

   183	              >
   184	                <ProductSection
   185	                  product={product}

   187	                />
   188	              </CenterMagnifyView>
   189	              {index < products.length - 1 ? <PageDivider /> : null}
   190	            </Fragment>
   191	          ))}
   192	        </View>
   193	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   194	    </View>
   195	  );

FILE: mob/app/contact.js

     1	import { Animated, Text, TextInput, View } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
     4	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
     5	import contactStyles from "../styles/contactStyles";
     6	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
     7	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
     8	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
     9	
    10	export default function ContactScreen() {
    11	  const {
    12	    compactTopLayout,
    13	    initialContentOffset,
    14	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    15	    scrollHandlers,
    16	    scrollY,
    17	  } =
    18	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    19	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    20	
    21	  return (
    22	    <View style={contactStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    23	      <Animated.ScrollView
    24	        style={contactStyles.scroll}
    25	        contentContainerStyle={[
    26	          contactStyles.scrollContent,
    27	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
    28	        ]}
    29	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
    30	        decelerationRate={0.95}
    31	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
    32	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
    33	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
    34	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
    35	        {...scrollHandlers}
    36	      >
    37	        <View style={contactStyles.main}>
    38	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
    39	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
    40	            pageTitleStyle={contactStyles.pageTitle}
    41	            scrollY={scrollY}

    89	            </CenterMagnifyView>
    90

    92	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Your Message</Text>
    93	              <TextInput
    94	                style={[contactStyles.input, contactStyles.messageInput]}

    96	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
    97	                multiline

    99	                textAlignVertical="top"
   100	              />
   101	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   102	
   103	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY} style={contactStyles.buttonWrap}>
   104	              <View style={contactStyles.button}>
   105	                <Text style={contactStyles.buttonText}>Send Message</Text>
   106	              </View>
   107	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   108	          </View>
   109	        </View>
   110	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   111	    </View>
   112	  );
   113	}

   279	function PiccolaQuantityTriangle({ direction, muted }) {
   280	  const strokeInset = piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth / 2;
   281	  const fillColor = muted ? "#FBDCB3" : "#f7b967";
   282	  const strokeColor = muted ? "#888888" : "#111111";
   283	  const path =
   284	    direction === "up"
   285	      ? [
   286	          `M ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${strokeInset}`,
   287	          `L ${
   288	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   289	          } ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset}`,
   290	          `L ${strokeInset} ${
   291	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   292	          }`,
   293	          "Z",
   294	        ].join(" ")
   295	      : [
   296	          `M ${strokeInset} ${strokeInset}`,
   297	          `L ${
   298	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   299	          } ${strokeInset}`,
   300	          `L ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${
   301	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   302	          }`,
   303	          "Z",
   304	        ].join(" ");
   305	
   306	  return (
   307	    <Svg
   308	      height="100%"
   309	      style={shopStyles.piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleSvg}
   310	      viewBox={`0 0 ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth} ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight}`}
   311	      width="100%"
   312	    >
   313	      <Path
   314	        d={path}
   315	        fill={fillColor}
   316	        stroke={strokeColor}
   317	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   318	        strokeWidth={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth}
   319	      />
   320	    </Svg>
   321	  );
   322	}

   365	          />
   366	        </Svg>

   493	    ? "Benefits"

   495	  const shippingPreviewActionAccessibilityLabel = isCartAddItemsActionVisible
   496	    ? "Add items"
   497	    : isDeliveryPaymentActionVisible
   498	    ? "Payment"
   499	    : isPaymentViewCartActionVisible
   500	    ? "View cart"
   501	    : isTruckOverlayVisible
   502	    ? "Benefits"
   503	    : "Open Piccola overlay";
   504	  const overlayNavBarResolvedWidth =
   505	    overlayNavBarWidth ||
   506	    Math.max(0, windowWidth - truckOverlayHorizontalMargin * 2);
   507	  const overlayNavItemWidth =
   508	    overlayNavBarResolvedWidth / overlayNavProducts.length;
   509	  const overlayNavIndicatorTranslateX = Animated.multiply(
   510	    overlayNavIndicatorProgress,
   511	    overlayNavItemWidth
   512	  );
   513	
   514	  const clearOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger = () => {
   515	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   516	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   517	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   518	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   519	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   520	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(0);
   521	  };
   522	
   523	  const startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger = (direction) => {
   524	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   525	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   526	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   527	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   528	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   529	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(1);
   530	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(
   531	      direction === "left" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   532	    );
   533	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(
   534	      direction === "right" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   535	    );
   536	  };
   537	
   538	  const showOverlayHeldArrows = () => {
   539	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   540	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
   541	  };
   542	
   543	  const hideOverlayHeldArrows = () => {
   544	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   545	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   546	  };
   547	
   548	  const overlayVisibleArrowOpacity = overlayHeldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   549	    inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   550	    outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   551	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   552	  });
   553	  const overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   554	    overlayVisibleArrowOpacity,
   555	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.interpolate({
   556	      inputRange: [0, 1],
   557	      outputRange: [1, 0],
   558	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   559	    })
   560	  );
   561	  const overlayLeftArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   562	    overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   563	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity
   564	  ).interpolate({
   565	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   566	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   567	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   568	  });
   569	  const overlayRightArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   570	    overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   571	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity
   572	  ).interpolate({
   573	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   574	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   575	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   576	  });
   577	  const overlayImageTravelDistance = Math.max(
   578	    overlayImageStageWidth / 2 + piccolaOverlayImageHalfSize,
   579	    150
   580	  );
   581	  const overlayIncomingStartOffset =
   582	    overlayImageDirection < 0
   583	      ? overlayImageTravelDistance
   584	      : -overlayImageTravelDistance;
   585	  const overlayOutgoingEndOffset = -overlayIncomingStartOffset;
   586	  const overlayIncomingImageTranslateX = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
   587	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   588	    outputRange: [overlayIncomingStartOffset, 0],
   589	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   590	  });
   591	  const overlayOutgoingImageTranslateX = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
   592	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   593	    outputRange: [0, overlayOutgoingEndOffset],
   594	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   595	  });
   596	  const overlayIncomingImageOpacity = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
   597	    inputRange: [0, 0.52, 0.82, 1],
   598	    outputRange: [0, 0.08, 0.68, 1],
   599	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   600	  });
   601	  const overlayOutgoingImageOpacity = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
   602	    inputRange: [0, 0.18, 0.36, 1],
   603	    outputRange: [1, 0.22, 0, 0],
   604	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   605	  });
   606	
   607	  const getOverlayProductTransitionDirection = (fromName, toName) => {
   608	    const fromIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
   609	      (product) => product.name === fromName
   610	    );
   611	    const toIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
   612	      (product) => product.name === toName
   613	    );
   614	
   615	    if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) {
   616	      return -1;
   617	    }
   618	
   619	    const lastOverlayIndex = overlayNavProducts.length - 1;
   620	
   621	    if (fromIndex === lastOverlayIndex && toIndex === 0) return -1;
   622	    if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastOverlayIndex) return 1;
   623	
   624	    return toIndex > fromIndex ? -1 : 1;
   625	  };
   626	
   627	  const transitionOverlayProductImage = (nextProductName, direction) => {
   628	    if (!nextProductName || nextProductName === activeOverlayProductName) {
   629	      return;
   630	    }
   631	
   632	    if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current) {
   633	      const previousAnimation = overlayImageAnimationRef.current;
   634	      overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
   635	      previousAnimation.stop();
   636	    }
   637	
   638	    if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   639	      const previousNavAnimation = overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current;
   640	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   641	      previousNavAnimation.stop();
   642	    }
   643	
   644	    const nextOverlayNavIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
   645	      (product) => product.name === nextProductName
   646	    );
   647	
   648	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(activeOverlayProductName);
   649	    setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(activeOverlayProductName);
   650	    setOverlayImageDirection(direction);
   651	    overlayImageProgress.setValue(0);
   652	    setActiveOverlayProductName(nextProductName);
   653	
   654	    if (nextOverlayNavIndex >= 0) {
   655	      const navAnimation = Animated.timing(overlayNavIndicatorProgress, {
   656	        toValue: nextOverlayNavIndex,
   657	        duration: 320,
   658	        easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
   659	        useNativeDriver: true,
   660	      });
   661	
   662	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = navAnimation;
   663	      navAnimation.start(() => {
   664	        if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current === navAnimation) {
   665	          overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   666	        }
   667	      });
   668	    }
   669	
   670	    const animation = Animated.timing(overlayImageProgress, {
   671	      toValue: 1,
   672	      duration: 320,
   673	      easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
   674	      useNativeDriver: true,
   675	    });
   676	
   677	    overlayImageAnimationRef.current = animation;
   678	
   679	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
   680	      if (finished) {
   681	        setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(null);
   682	      }
   683	
   684	      if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current === animation) {
   685	        overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
   686	      }
   687	    });
   688	  };
   689	
   690	  const handleOverlayProductNameSelect = (nextProductName) => {
   691	    const direction = getOverlayProductTransitionDirection(
   692	      activeOverlayProductName,
   693	      nextProductName
   694	    );
   695	
   696	    transitionOverlayProductImage(nextProductName, direction);
   697	  };
   698	
   699	  const renderOverlayArrowChevron = (direction, muted = false) => (
   700	    <View
   701	      style={[
   702	        shopStyles.overlayImageArrowChevron,
   703	        muted && shopStyles.overlayImageArrowChevronMuted,
   704	        direction === "left"
   705	          ? shopStyles.overlayImageArrowChevronLeft
   706	          : shopStyles.overlayImageArrowChevronRight,
   707	      ]}
   708	    />
   709	  );
   710	
   711	  const goToOverlayPreviousProduct = () => {
   712	    const currentIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
   713	      (product) => product.name === activeOverlayProductName
   714	    );
   715	    if (currentIndex < 0) return;
   716	
   717	    const previousIndex =
   718	      (currentIndex + overlayNavProducts.length - 1) %
   719	      overlayNavProducts.length;
   720	    startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   721	    transitionOverlayProductImage(overlayNavProducts[previousIndex].name, 1);
   722	  };
   723	
   724	  const goToOverlayNextProduct = () => {
   725	    const currentIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
   726	      (product) => product.name === activeOverlayProductName
   727	    );
   728	    if (currentIndex < 0) return;
   729	
   730	    const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % overlayNavProducts.length;
   731	    startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   732	    transitionOverlayProductImage(overlayNavProducts[nextIndex].name, -1);
   733	  };
   734	
   735	  const getOverlayBandTouchPoint = (event) => {
   736	    const primaryTouch =
   737	      event?.nativeEvent?.touches?.[0] ||
   738	      event?.nativeEvent?.changedTouches?.[0] ||
   739	      event?.nativeEvent;
   740	
   741	    return {
   742	      x:
   743	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageX === "number"
   744	          ? primaryTouch.pageX
   745	          : primaryTouch?.locationX,
   746	      y:
   747	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageY === "number"
   748	          ? primaryTouch.pageY
   749	          : primaryTouch?.locationY,
   750	    };
   751	  };
   752	
   753	  const handleOverlayBandTouchStart = (event) => {
   754	    const { x, y } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
   755	
   756	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = x;
   757	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = y;
   758	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = false;
   759	    showOverlayHeldArrows();
   760	  };
   761	
   762	  const handleOverlayBandTouchMove = (event) => {
   763	    if (overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current) return;
   764	
   765	    const startX = overlaySwipeStartXRef.current;
   766	    const startY = overlaySwipeStartYRef.current;
   767	    const { x: currentX, y: currentY } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
   768	
   769	    if (
   770	      typeof startX !== "number" ||
   771	      typeof startY !== "number" ||
   772	      typeof currentX !== "number" ||
   773	      typeof currentY !== "number"
   774	    ) {
   775	      return;
   776	    }
   777	
   778	    const dx = currentX - startX;
   779	    const dy = Math.abs(currentY - startY);
   780	    const minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance = 12.5;
   781	    const horizontalDominanceRatio = 0.4375;
   782	    const isHorizontalSwipeIntent =
   783	      Math.abs(dx) >= minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance &&
   784	      Math.abs(dx) > dy * horizontalDominanceRatio;
   785	
   786	    if (!isHorizontalSwipeIntent) return;
   787	
   788	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = true;
   789	
   790	    if (dx < 0) {
   791	      goToOverlayNextProduct();
   792	    } else {
   793	      goToOverlayPreviousProduct();
   794	    }
   795	  };
   796	
   797	  const handleOverlayBandTouchEnd = () => {
   798	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = null;
   799	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = null;
   800	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = false;
   801	    hideOverlayHeldArrows();
   802	  };
   803	
   804	  useEffect(() => {
   805	    if (overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current) {
   806	      clearTimeout(overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current);
   807	    }
   808	
   809	    overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   810	      clearOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger();
   811	      overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current = null;
   812	    }, 130);
   813	
   814	    return () => {
   815	      if (overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current) {
   816	        clearTimeout(overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current);
   817	      }
   818	    };
   819	  }, [activeOverlayProductName]);
   820	
   821	  useEffect(() => {
   822	    return () => {
   823	      if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current) {
   824	        overlayImageAnimationRef.current.stop();
   825	      }
   826	
   827	      if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   828	        overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current.stop();
   829	      }
   830	    };
   831	  }, []);
   832	  const shippingPreviewSofloBottomY =
   833	    shippingPreviewMeasurements.rowY +
   834	    shippingPreviewMeasurements.sofloY +
   835	    shippingPreviewMeasurements.sofloHeight +
   836	    shippingPreviewSofloVisualOffsetY;

   840	    shippingPreviewAvailableBottomY -

   846	    shippingPreviewMeasurements.sofloHeight;
   847	  const shippingPreviewStickyCartAlignedMarginTop = Math.max(
   848	    0,

   890	    : null;
   891	  const shippingPreviewGoBackButtonLeft =
   892	    (windowWidth - shippingPreviewReadyButtonWidth) / 2;
   893	  const truckOverlayVerticalGap = 24;

   895	    shopMainPaddingTop +

   897	    truckOverlayVerticalGap;

   899	    shopMainPaddingTop + shippingPreviewReadyButtonTopY;
   900	  const truckOverlayTop = truckOverlayVerticalGap;
   901	  const truckOverlayBottom =

   904	    120,

   907	  const truckOverlayPreviousHeight = Math.max(

   911	  const truckOverlayRawContentOffsetTop = Math.max(
   912	    0,

   916	    0,

   928	  );
   929	  const piccolaOverlayInnerWidth =
   930	    windowWidth -

   933	    truckOverlayInnerHorizontalPadding * 2;

   968	    piccolaOverlayQuantityTopBoxHeight;
   969	  const cartOverlayProductTop =
   970	    overlayOrangeBandHeight + piccolaOverlayHeadingTopPadding;
   971	  const cartOverlayCreamHorizontalInset = truckOverlayInnerHorizontalPadding;
   972	  const cartOverlayCreamVerticalInset = cartOverlayCreamHorizontalInset;

  1046	  const cartOverlayDeliveryTotal =

  1054	  const updateShippingPreviewMeasurement = (key, value) => {
  1055	    setShippingPreviewMeasurements((current) => {
  1056	      if (
  1057	        typeof current[key] === "number" &&
  1058	        Math.abs(current[key] - value) < 0.5
  1059	      ) {
  1060	        return current;
  1061	      }
  1062	
  1063	      return { ...current, [key]: value };
  1064	    });
  1065	  };
  1066	  const updatePiccolaOverlayDescriptionHeight = (height) => {
  1067	    setPiccolaOverlayDescriptionHeight((current) => {
  1068	      if (Math.abs(current - height) < 0.5) return current;
  1069	
  1070	      return height;
  1071	    });
  1072	  };
  1073	
  1074	  const openTruckOverlay = () => {
  1075	    if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
  1076	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current.stop();
  1077	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  1078	    }
  1079	
  1080	    overlayNavIndicatorProgress.setValue(initialOverlayNavIndex);
  1081	    setActiveOverlayProductName(piccolaProduct.name);
  1082	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(false);
  1083	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);
  1084	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  1085	    setIsShopOverlayVisible(true);
  1086	    setIsTruckOverlayVisible(true);
  1087	  };
  1088	  const closeTruckOverlay = () => {
  1089	    if (isCartOverlayVisible) {
  1090	      pruneZeroQuantityCartEntries();
  1091	    }
  1092	
  1093	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(activeOverlayProductName);
  1094	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(false);
  1095	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);
  1096	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  1097	    setIsShopOverlayVisible(false);
  1098	    setIsTruckOverlayVisible(false);
  1099	  };
  1100	  const toggleTruckOverlay = () => {
  1101	    if (isTruckOverlayVisible) {
  1102	      if (isCartOverlayVisible) {
  1103	        pruneZeroQuantityCartEntries();
  1104	        setIsCartOverlayVisible(false);
  1105	        return;
  1106	      }
  1107	
  1108	      closeTruckOverlay();
  1109	      return;
  1110	    }
  1111	
  1112	    openTruckOverlay();
  1113	  };
  1114	  const showProductOverlayFromCart = () => {
  1115	    pruneZeroQuantityCartEntries();
  1116	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(false);
  1117	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);
  1118	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  1119	  };
  1120	  const showDeliveryOverlayFromCart = () => {
  1121	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") {
  1122	      return;
  1123	    }
  1124	
  1125	    pruneZeroQuantityCartEntries();
  1126	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(false);
  1127	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(true);
  1128	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  1129	  };
  1130	  const showPaymentOverlayFromDelivery = () => {
  1131	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") {
  1132	      return;
  1133	    }
  1134	
  1135	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);
  1136	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(true);
  1137	  };
  1138	  const showCartOverlayFromPayment = () => {
  1139	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") {
  1140	      return;
  1141	    }
  1142	
  1143	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  1144	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(true);
  1145	  };
  1146	  const handleShippingPreviewActionPress = () => {
  1147	    if (isCartAddItemsActionVisible) {
  1148	      showProductOverlayFromCart();
  1149	      return;
  1150	    }
  1151	
  1152	    if (isDeliveryPaymentActionVisible) {
  1153	      showPaymentOverlayFromDelivery();
  1154	      return;
  1155	    }
  1156	
  1157	    if (isPaymentViewCartActionVisible) {
  1158	      showCartOverlayFromPayment();
  1159	      return;
  1160	    }
  1161	
  1162	    toggleTruckOverlay();
  1163	  };
  1164	
  1165	  useEffect(() => {
  1166	    setIsShopOverlayVisible(isTruckOverlayVisible);
  1167	
  1168	    return () => {
  1169	      setIsShopOverlayVisible(false);
  1170	    };
  1171	  }, [isTruckOverlayVisible, setIsShopOverlayVisible]);
  1172	
  1173	  useEffect(() => {
  1174	    if (!cartOverlayActionRequest.pending) return;
  1175	
  1176	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(activeOverlayProductName);
  1177	    setIsShopOverlayVisible(true);
  1178	    setIsTruckOverlayVisible(true);
  1179	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);
  1180	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  1181	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(true);
  1182	    consumeCartOverlayActionRequest(cartOverlayActionRequest.id);

  1184	    activeOverlayProductName,
  1185	    cartOverlayActionRequest,
  1186	    consumeCartOverlayActionRequest,
  1187	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft,
  1188	    setIsShopOverlayVisible,
  1189	  ]);
  1190

  1193	
  1194	    if (
  1195	      !openCartRequest ||
  1196	      handledOpenCartParamRef.current === openCartRequest
  1197	    ) {
  1198	      return;
  1199	    }
  1200

  1202	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(activeOverlayProductName);
  1203	    setIsShopOverlayVisible(true);
  1204	    setIsTruckOverlayVisible(true);
  1205	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);
  1206	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  1207	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(true);
  1208	  }, [
  1209	    activeOverlayProductName,
  1210	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft,
  1211	    openCart,
  1212	    setIsShopOverlayVisible,
  1213	  ]);
  1214

  1217	      return undefined;

  1220	    const subscription = BackHandler.addEventListener(
  1221	      "hardwareBackPress",
  1222	      () => {
  1223	        closeTruckOverlay();
  1224	        return true;
  1225	      }
  1226	    );
  1227	
  1228	    return () => subscription.remove();
  1229	  }, [
  1230	    activeOverlayProductName,
  1231	    isTruckOverlayVisible,
  1232	    overlayProductConfirmations,
  1233	  ]);

  2440	                        </View>
  2441	                      </View>

  2445	              </View>
  2446	            </View>
  2447	          </View>
  2448	        </View>
  2449	      ) : null}
  2450	
  2451	      {isTruckOverlayVisible

  2461	        : null}

  2463	    </View>
  2464	  );

    74	const carouselSwipeActivationRatio = Platform.OS === "ios" ? 1.5 : 1.05;

    77	const pageLabels = {

    93	function getActivePageFromPath(pathname) {
    94	  if (pathname === "/products") return "products";
    95	  if (pathname === "/aboutus") return "aboutus";
    96	  if (pathname === "/contact") return "contact";
    97	  if (pathname === "/shop") return "shop";
    98	  return "home";
    99	}
   100	
   101	function getNavigationDirection(fromPage, toPage) {
   102	  const fromIndex = navPages.indexOf(fromPage);
   103	  const toIndex = navPages.indexOf(toPage);
   104	
   105	  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) return 1;
   106	
   107	  const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   108	
   109	  if (fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0) return 1;
   110	  if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex) return -1;
   111	
   112	  return toIndex > fromIndex ? 1 : -1;
   113	}
