   115	export default function AppHeader({
   116	  activePage,
   117	  dimHeaderExceptShopButton = false,
   118	  scrollY,
   119	  showCarousel = true,
   120	  showHero = true,
   121	  showOnlyCarousel = false,
   122	  showOnlyHero = false,
   123	}) {
   124	  const pathname = usePathname();
   125	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   126	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   127	  const resolvedActivePage = activePage || getActivePageFromPath(pathname);
   128	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   129	  const activePageIndex = Math.max(navPages.indexOf(resolvedActivePage), 0);
   130	  const handlesCarouselVisuals = showCarousel && !showOnlyHero;
   131	
   132	  const fallbackScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   133	  const safeScrollY = scrollY || fallbackScrollY;
   134	  const canNavigateWithHeader = useHeaderNavigationGate(safeScrollY);
   135	  const heroStateScrollY =
   136	    showHero && backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen && backgroundHeroState?.heroScrollY
   137	      ? backgroundHeroState.heroScrollY
   138	      : safeScrollY;
   139	  const headerMotionScrollY = showOnlyHero ? heroStateScrollY : safeScrollY;
   140	
   141	  const fallbackHeldArrowOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   142	  const fallbackRouteTransitionProgress = useRef(
   143	    new Animated.Value(0)
   144	  ).current;
   145	  const heldArrowOpacity =
   146	    screenSwipe?.heldArrowOpacity || fallbackHeldArrowOpacity;
   147	  const routeTransitionProgress =
   148	    screenSwipe?.routeTransitionProgress || fallbackRouteTransitionProgress;
   149	  const indicatorProgress = useRef(new Animated.Value(activePageIndex)).current;
   150	  const fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity = useRef(
   151	    new Animated.Value(0)
   152	  ).current;
   153	  const fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity = useRef(
   154	    new Animated.Value(0)
   155	  ).current;
   156	  const fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression = useRef(
   157	    new Animated.Value(0)
   158	  ).current;
   159	  const directionalLeftArrowOpacity =
   160	    screenSwipe?.directionalLeftArrowOpacity ||
   161	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity;
   162	  const directionalRightArrowOpacity =
   163	    screenSwipe?.directionalRightArrowOpacity ||
   164	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity;
   165	  const directionalArrowBaseSuppression =
   166	    screenSwipe?.directionalArrowBaseSuppression ||
   167	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression;
   168	
   169	  const indicatorAnimationRef = useRef(null);
   170	  const indicatorIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   171	  const suppressCarouselPressUntilRef = useRef(0);
   172	  const canNavigateWithHeaderRef = useRef(canNavigateWithHeader);
   173	
   174	  const activePageRef = useRef(resolvedActivePage);
   175	  const activeIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   176	  canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current = canNavigateWithHeader;
   177	  activePageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
   178	  activeIndexRef.current = activePageIndex;
   179	
   180	  useEffect(() => {
   181	    if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return;
   182	
   183	    const resolvedCheeseboardImage = Image.resolveAssetSource(
   184	      cheeseboardProductsImage
   185	    );
   186	
   187	    if (resolvedCheeseboardImage?.uri) {
   188	      Image.prefetch(resolvedCheeseboardImage.uri).catch(() => {});
   189	    }
   190	  }, []);
   191	
   192	  const showHeldArrows = (event = null) => {
   193	    if (screenSwipe?.showHeldArrowHint) {
   194	      screenSwipe.showHeldArrowHint(event);
   195	      return;
   196	    }
   197	
   198	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   199	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
   200	  };
   201	
   202	  const hideHeldArrows = () => {
   203	    if (screenSwipe?.hideHeldArrowHint) {
   204	      screenSwipe.hideHeldArrowHint();
   205	      return;
   206	    }
   207	
   208	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   209	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   210	  };
   211	
   212	  const cancelHeldArrows = () => {
   213	    if (screenSwipe?.cancelHeldArrowHint) {
   214	      screenSwipe.cancelHeldArrowHint();
   215	      return;
   216	    }
   217	
   218	    hideHeldArrows();
   219	  };
   220	
   221	  const updateHeldArrowsFromTouchMove = (event) => {
   222	    screenSwipe?.updateHeldArrowHintMovement?.(event);
   223	    return false;
   224	  };
   225	
   226	  const clearDirectionalArrowLinger = () => {
   227	    if (screenSwipe?.clearDirectionalArrowLinger) {
   228	      screenSwipe.clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   229	      return;
   230	    }
   231	
   232	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   233	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   234	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   235	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   236	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   237	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(0);
   238	  };
   239	
   240	  const startDirectionalArrowLinger = (direction) => {
   241	    if (screenSwipe?.startDirectionalArrowLinger) {
   242	      screenSwipe.startDirectionalArrowLinger(direction);
   243	      return;
   244	    }
   245	
   246	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   247	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   248	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   249	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(1);
   250	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(
   251	      direction === "left" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   252	    );
   253	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(
   254	      direction === "right" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   255	    );
   256	  };
   257	
   258	  const updateSwipePreview = (dragDistance) => {
   259	    if (!screenSwipe) return;
   260	
   261	    screenSwipe.updateSwipe({
   262	      x: dragDistance,
   263	    });
   264	  };
   265	
   266	  const animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes = (fromIndex, toIndex) => {
   267	    if (fromIndex === toIndex) {
   268	      if (!indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   269	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   270	      }
   271	      return;
   272	    }
   273	
   274	    if (indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   275	      const previousAnimation = indicatorAnimationRef.current;
   276	      indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   277	      previousAnimation.stop();
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   281	
   282	    const createIndicatorTiming = (toValue, duration = indicatorSlideDuration) =>
   283	      Animated.timing(indicatorProgress, {
   284	        toValue,
   285	        duration,
   286	        useNativeDriver: true,
   287	      });
   288	
   289	    const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   290	    const animation =
   291	      fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0
   292	        ? Animated.sequence([
   293	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length),
   294	            createIndicatorTiming(-1, 0),
   295	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   296	          ])
   297	        : fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex
   298	        ? Animated.sequence([
   299	            createIndicatorTiming(-1),
   300	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length, 0),
   301	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   302	          ])
   303	        : createIndicatorTiming(toIndex);
   304	
   305	    indicatorAnimationRef.current = animation;
   306	
   307	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
   308	      if (finished) {
   309	        indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   310	      }
   311	
   312	      if (indicatorAnimationRef.current === animation) {
   313	        indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   314	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   315	      }
   316	    });
   317	  };
   318	
   319	  useEffect(() => {
   320	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(indicatorIndexRef.current, activePageIndex);
   321	  }, [activePageIndex, indicatorProgress]);
   322	
   323	  const animateIndicatorToPage = (pageName) => {
   324	    const pageIndex = navPages.indexOf(pageName);
   325	    if (pageIndex < 0) return;
   326	
   327	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(activeIndexRef.current, pageIndex);
   328	  };
   329	
   330	  const goToPage = (pageName, animateIndicator = true) => {
   331	    hideHeldArrows();
   332	
   333	    if (pageName === activePageRef.current) {
   334	      if (pageName === "shop") {
   335	        goToPage("home", animateIndicator);
   336	      }
   337	
   338	      return;
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    const route = pageRoutes[pageName];
   342	    if (!route) return;
   343	
   344	    if (navPages.includes(pageName)) {
   345	      if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   346	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   347	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   348	        return;
   349	      }
   350	
   351	      backgroundHeroState?.freezeHero(safeScrollY);
   352	      if (animateIndicator) animateIndicatorToPage(pageName);
   353	      router.replace(route);
   354	      return;
   355	    }
   356	
   357	    router.push(route);
   358	  };
   359	
   360	  const goToPreviousPage = () => {
   361	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   362	
   363	    const previousIndex =
   364	      (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   365	
   366	    goToPage(navPages[previousIndex], true, -1);
   367	  };
   368	
   369	  const goToNextPage = () => {
   370	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   371	
   372	    const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   373	
   374	    goToPage(navPages[nextIndex], true, 1);
   375	  };
   376	
   377	  const shouldClaimCarouselSwipe = (_, gestureState) => {
   378	    if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return false;
   379	
   380	    const horizontalDistance = Math.abs(gestureState.dx);
   381	    const verticalDistance = Math.abs(gestureState.dy);
   382	
   383	    if (
   384	      horizontalDistance >= tapHoldHorizontalCancelDistance &&
   385	      horizontalDistance > verticalDistance * tapHoldHorizontalDominanceRatio
   386	    ) {
   387	      cancelHeldArrows();
   388	    }
   389	
   390	    const shouldClaim =
   391	      horizontalDistance > carouselSwipeActivationDistance &&
   392	      horizontalDistance > verticalDistance * carouselSwipeActivationRatio;
   393	
   394	    if (shouldClaim) {
   395	      cancelHeldArrows();
   396	    }
   397	
   398	    return shouldClaim;
   399	  };
   400	
   401	  const carouselPanResponder = useRef(
   402	    PanResponder.create({
   403	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: shouldClaimCarouselSwipe,
   404	
   405	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: shouldClaimCarouselSwipe,
   406	
   407	      onPanResponderTerminationRequest: (_, gestureState) =>
   408	        !shouldClaimCarouselSwipe(null, gestureState),
   409	
   410	      onPanResponderMove: (_, gestureState) => {
   411	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return;
   412	
   413	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   414	        updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   415	
   416	        if (gestureState.dx <= -carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   417	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   418	          return;
   419	        }
   420	
   421	        if (gestureState.dx >= carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   422	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   423	          return;
   424	        }
   425	
   426	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   427	      },
   428	
   429	      onPanResponderRelease: (_, gestureState) => {
   430	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   431	        hideHeldArrows();
   432	
   433	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   434	          clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   435	          screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   436	          return;
   437	        }
   438	
   439	        if (gestureState.dx <= -carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   440	          const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   441	          const nextPage = navPages[nextIndex];
   442	
   443	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   444	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   445	            x: gestureState.dx,
   446	            page: nextPage,
   447	            direction: 1,
   448	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   449	          });
   450	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   451	          goToPage(nextPage, true, 1);
   452	
   453	          return;
   454	        }
   455	
   456	        if (gestureState.dx >= carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   457	          const previousIndex =
   458	            (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   459	          const previousPage = navPages[previousIndex];
   460	
   461	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   462	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   463	            x: gestureState.dx,
   464	            page: previousPage,
   465	            direction: -1,
   466	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   467	          });
   468	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   469	          goToPage(previousPage, true, -1);
   470	
   471	          return;
   472	        }
   473	
   474	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   475	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   476	      },
   477	
   478	      onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
   479	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   480	        hideHeldArrows();
   481	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   482	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   483	      },
   484	
   485	      onShouldBlockNativeResponder: () => true,
   486	    })
   487	  ).current;
   488	
   489	  useEffect(() => {
   490	    hideHeldArrows();
   491	
   492	    return () => {
   493	      hideHeldArrows();
   494	    };
   495	  }, [resolvedActivePage, showCarousel, showOnlyHero]);
   496	
   497	  useEffect(
   498	    () => () => {
   499	      clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   500	    },
   501	    []
   502	  );
   503	
   504	  const activeLink = pageLabels[resolvedActivePage] || "Home";
   505	  const arrowLinkTranslateX = 0;
   506	
   507	  const scrollBeganArrowVisibility = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   508	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   509	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   510	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   511	  });
   512	  const oldHeaderArrowVisibility = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   513	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   514	    outputRange: [1, 0],
   515	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   516	  });
   517	  const oldHeaderHeldArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   518	    heldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   519	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   520	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   521	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   522	    }),
   523	    oldHeaderArrowVisibility
   524	  );
   525	  const scrolledStateArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   526	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility,
   527	    arrowHintPeakOpacity
   528	  );
   529	  const scrolledVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   530	    heldArrowOpacity,
   531	    scrolledStateArrowOpacity
   532	  );
   533	  const gatedScrolledArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   534	    scrolledVisibleArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   535	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   536	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   537	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   538	    }),
   539	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility
   540	  );
   541	  const gatedVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   542	    oldHeaderHeldArrowOpacity,
   543	    gatedScrolledArrowOpacity
   544	  ).interpolate({
   545	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   546	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   547	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   548	  });
   549	  const directionalBaseArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   550	    gatedVisibleArrowOpacity,
   551	    directionalArrowBaseSuppression.interpolate({
   552	      inputRange: [0, 1],
   553	      outputRange: [1, 0],
   554	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   555	    })
   556	  );
   557	  const leftArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   558	    directionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   559	    directionalLeftArrowOpacity
   560	  ).interpolate({
   561	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   562	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   563	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   564	  });
   565	  const rightArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   566	    directionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   567	    directionalRightArrowOpacity
   568	  ).interpolate({
   569	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   570	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   571	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   572	  });
   573	
   574	  const heroVerticalFadeTranslateY = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   575	    inputRange: [0, heroVerticalFadeScrollDistance],
   576	    outputRange: [heroVerticalFadeHeight, -heroVerticalFadeFeatherHeight],
   577	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   578	  });
   579	
   580	  const stickyOffset =
   581	    Platform.OS === "web"
   582	      ? 0
   583	      : headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   584	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   585	          outputRange: [0, carouselStickyOffsetY],
   586	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   587	        });
   588	  const carouselExpansionOffsetY =
   589	    Platform.OS === "ios"
   590	      ? headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   591	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   592	          outputRange: [0, carouselExpansionHeight],
   593	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   594	        })
   595	      : stickyOffset;
   596	  const carouselInnerTranslateY = carouselExpansionOffsetY;
   597	
   598	  const centerShadowOpacity =
   599	    Platform.OS === "web"
   600	      ? 1
   601	      : headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   602	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   603	          outputRange: [0, 1],
   604	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   605	        });
   606	
   607	  const stickyExpansionTranslateY = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   608	    inputRange: [96, 120],
   609	    outputRange: [-stickyExpansionMaxHeight, 0],
   610	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   611	  });
   612	
   613	  const heroScale = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   614	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   615	    outputRange: [heroStartScale, heroScaleAtMinimumOpacity],
   616	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   617	  });
   618	
   619	  const heroTranslateY = heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   620	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   621	    outputRange: [heroStartTranslateY, heroTranslateYAtMinimumOpacity],
   622	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   623	  });
   624	
   625	  const headerTranslateY = headerMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   626	    inputRange: [0, 120],
   627	    outputRange: [0, -120],
   628	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   629	  });
   630	
   631	  const indicatorSegmentWidth = windowWidth / navPages.length;
   632	  const indicatorTranslateX = Animated.multiply(
   633	    indicatorProgress,
   634	    indicatorSegmentWidth
   635	  );
   636	  const isMainHeroPage = navPages.includes(resolvedActivePage);
   637	  const heroImageSource = isMainHeroPage
   638	    ? noCheeseboardHeroImage
   639	    : defaultHeroImage;
   640	  const mainHeroBackgroundFadeOpacity = isMainHeroPage
   641	    ? heroStateScrollY.interpolate({
   642	        inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   643	        outputRange: [0, heroVerticalFadeOverlayOpacity],
   644	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   645	      })
   646	    : 1;
   647	  const heroWidthFitScale = windowWidth / heroSourceWidth;
   648	  const heroWidthFitImageHeight = heroSourceHeight * heroWidthFitScale;
   649	  const heroImageFrameStyle = isMainHeroPage
   650	    ? {
   651	        width: windowWidth,
   652	        height: heroWidthFitImageHeight,
   653	      }
   654	    : null;
   655	  const heroImageTransformStyle = isMainHeroPage
   656	    ? null
   657	    : {
   658	        transform: [{ scale: heroScale }, { translateY: heroTranslateY }],
   659	      };
   660	  const heroCoverScale = Math.max(
   661	    windowWidth / heroSourceWidth,
   662	    heroVerticalFadeHeight / heroSourceHeight
   663	  );
   664	  const heroLayoutScale = isMainHeroPage ? heroWidthFitScale : heroCoverScale;
   665	  const heroRenderedWidth = heroSourceWidth * heroLayoutScale;
   666	  const heroRenderedHeight = heroSourceHeight * heroLayoutScale;
   667	  const heroRenderedLeft = (windowWidth - heroRenderedWidth) / 2;
   668	  const heroRenderedTop = isMainHeroPage
   669	    ? 0
   670	    : (heroVerticalFadeHeight - heroRenderedHeight) / 2;
   671	  const noCheeseboardVisibleBottomY = Math.min(
   672	    heroVerticalFadeHeight,
   673	    heroRenderedTop + noCheeseboardVisibleBottomSourceY * heroLayoutScale
   674	  );
   675	  const cheeseboardWidth = cheeseboardSourceWidth * heroLayoutScale;
   676	  const cheeseboardHeight = cheeseboardSourceHeight * heroLayoutScale;
   677	  const cheeseboardLeft =
   678	    heroRenderedLeft + cheeseboardSourceX * heroLayoutScale;
   679	  const cheeseboardTop = isMainHeroPage
   680	    ? noCheeseboardVisibleBottomY - cheeseboardHeight / 2
   681	    : heroRenderedTop + cheeseboardSourceY * heroLayoutScale;
   682	  const routeTransitionState = screenSwipe?.routeTransitionState;
   683	  const isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled =
   684	    isMainHeroPage && isHeaderNewState(heroStateScrollY);
   685	  const isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive =
   686	    isMainHeroPage &&
   687	    !isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled &&
   688	    Boolean(routeTransitionState?.isActive);
   689	  const isCheeseboardSwipeActive =
   690	    isMainHeroPage &&
   691	    !isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled &&
   692	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive);
   693	  const cheeseboardTransitionDirection =
   694	    routeTransitionState?.direction === -1 ? -1 : 1;
   695	  const cheeseboardTransitionStartX =
   696	    typeof routeTransitionState?.startX === "number"
   697	      ? routeTransitionState.startX
   698	      : 0;
   699	  const cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX =
   700	    Platform.OS === "ios" ? 0.01 : 0;
   701	  const cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX =
   702	    Math.abs(cheeseboardTransitionStartX) > 0.01
   703	      ? cheeseboardTransitionStartX
   704	      : cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX;
   705	  const cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity =
   706	    Platform.OS === "ios" ? 0.001 : 0;
   707	  const cheeseboardDragTranslateX = isCheeseboardSwipeActive
   708	    ? screenSwipe?.swipeX || 0
   709	    : 0;
   710	  const cheeseboardBaseTranslateX = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   711	    ? 0
   712	    : cheeseboardDragTranslateX;
   713	  const cheeseboardBaseOpacity = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   714	    ? routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   715	        inputRange: [0, 0.92, 1],
   716	        outputRange: [
   717	          cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity,
   718	          cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity,
   719	          1,
   720	        ],
   721	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   722	      })
   723	    : 1;
   724	  const cheeseboardOutgoingTranslateX = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   725	    ? routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   726	        inputRange: [0, 1],
   727	        outputRange: [
   728	          cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX,
   729	          -cheeseboardTransitionDirection * windowWidth,
   730	        ],
   731	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   732	      })
   733	    : cheeseboardDragTranslateX;
   734	  const cheeseboardIncomingTranslateX = routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   735	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   736	    outputRange: [
   737	      cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX +
   738	        cheeseboardTransitionDirection * windowWidth,
   739	      cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX,
   740	    ],
   741	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   742	  });
   743	  const cheeseboardIncomingOpacity = routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   744	    inputRange: [0, 0.92, 1],
   745	    outputRange: [1, 1, 0],
   746	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   747	  });
   748	  const cheeseboardPreviousPreviewTranslateX = screenSwipe?.swipeX
   749	    ? Animated.add(screenSwipe.swipeX, -windowWidth)
   750	    : -windowWidth;
   751	  const cheeseboardNextPreviewTranslateX = screenSwipe?.swipeX
   752	    ? Animated.add(screenSwipe.swipeX, windowWidth)
   753	    : windowWidth;
   754	  const shouldShowCheeseboardDragPreviews =
   755	    isMainHeroPage &&
   756	    isCheeseboardSwipeActive &&
   757	    !isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive;
   758	  const handleCarouselTouchStart = (event) => {
   759	    showHeldArrows(event);
   760	    return false;
   761	  };
   762	
   763	  const sharedHeaderTouchHandlers = {
   764	    onStartShouldSetResponderCapture: handleCarouselTouchStart,
   765	    onTouchStart: showHeldArrows,
   766	    onTouchMove: updateHeldArrowsFromTouchMove,
   767	    onTouchEnd: hideHeldArrows,
   768	    onTouchCancel: hideHeldArrows,
   769	  };
   770	
   771	  const renderArrowChevron = (direction) => (
   772	    <View
   773	      style={[
   774	        styles.arrowChevron,
   775	        direction === "left"
   776	          ? styles.arrowChevronLeft
   777	          : styles.arrowChevronRight,
   778	      ]}
   779	    />
   780	  );
   781	
   782	  const renderCheeseboardLayer = (key, translateX, opacity = 1) => {
   783	    const slideTransformStyle =
   784	      translateX === 0 ? null : { transform: [{ translateX }] };
   785	    const slideOpacityStyle = opacity === 1 ? null : { opacity };
   786	
   787	    return (
   788	      <Animated.View
   789	        key={key}
   790	        collapsable={false}
   791	        pointerEvents="none"
   792	        renderToHardwareTextureAndroid
   793	        style={[
   794	          styles.cheeseboardSlideLayer,
   795	          slideTransformStyle,
   796	          slideOpacityStyle,
   797	        ]}
   798	      >
   799	        <Animated.View
   800	          collapsable={false}
   801	          style={styles.cheeseboardHeroTransformLayer}
   802	        >
   803	          <Animated.View
   804	            collapsable={false}
   805	            renderToHardwareTextureAndroid
   806	            style={[
   807	              styles.cheeseboardProductsFrame,
   808	              {
   809	                left: cheeseboardLeft,
   810	                top: cheeseboardTop,
   811	                width: cheeseboardWidth,
   812	                height: cheeseboardHeight,
   813	                transform: [{ scale: heroScale }],
   814	              },
   815	            ]}
   816	          >
   817	            <Image
   818	              fadeDuration={0}
   819	              source={cheeseboardProductsImage}
   820	              style={styles.cheeseboardProductsImage}
   821	              resizeMode="contain"
   822	            />
   823	          </Animated.View>
   824	        </Animated.View>
   825	      </Animated.View>
   826	    );
   827	  };
   828	
   829	  const carousel = (
   830	    <View
   831	      style={styles.carouselShell}
   832	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
   833	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
   834	    >
   835	      <Animated.View style={styles.carouselNavBar}>
   836	        <View style={styles.carouselStickyExpansion}>
   837	          <Animated.View
   838	            pointerEvents="none"
   839	            style={[
   840	              styles.carouselStickyExpansionFill,
   841	              {
   842	                transform: [{ translateY: stickyExpansionTranslateY }],
   843	              },
   844	            ]}
   845	          />
   846	        </View>
   847	
   848	        <Animated.View
   849	          style={[
   850	            styles.carouselInner,
   851	            {
   852	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselInnerTranslateY }],
   853	            },
   854	          ]}
   855	        >
   856	          <Pressable
   857	            onPress={goToPreviousPage}
   858	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   859	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   860	          >
   861	            <Animated.View
   862	              style={[
   863	                styles.arrowBox,
   864	                {
   865	                  opacity: leftArrowOpacity,
   866	                  transform: [
   867	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   868	                  ],
   869	                },
   870	              ]}
   871	            >
   872	              {renderArrowChevron("left")}
   873	            </Animated.View>
   874	          </Pressable>
   875	
   876	          <Pressable
   877	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   878	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   879	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   880	            style={styles.carouselActiveWrap}
   881	          >
   882	            <Text
   883	              numberOfLines={1}
   884	              adjustsFontSizeToFit
   885	              minimumFontScale={0.88}
   886	              style={[
   887	                styles.carouselActiveText,
   888	                styles.carouselActiveTextLayer,
   889	                {
   890	                  transform: [
   891	                    { translateY: activeTextBaseOffsetY },
   892	                  ],
   893	                },
   894	              ]}
   895	            >
   896	              {activeLink}
   897	            </Text>
   898	          </Pressable>
   899	
   900	          <Pressable
   901	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   902	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   903	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   904	          >
   905	            <Animated.View
   906	              style={[
   907	                styles.arrowBox,
   908	                {
   909	                  opacity: rightArrowOpacity,
   910	                  transform: [
   911	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   912	                  ],
   913	                },
   914	              ]}
   915	            >
   916	              {renderArrowChevron("right")}
   917	            </Animated.View>
   918	          </Pressable>
   919	        </Animated.View>
   920	
   921	        <Animated.View
   922	          pointerEvents="none"
   923	          style={[
   924	            styles.carouselIndicatorSeparator,
   925	            {
   926	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
   927	            },
   928	          ]}
   929	        />
   930	
   931	        <Animated.View
   932	          pointerEvents="none"
   933	          style={[
   934	            styles.carouselIndicatorBar,
   935	            {
   936	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
   937	            },
   938	          ]}
   939	        >
   940	          <View style={styles.carouselIndicatorTrack}>
   941	            {navPages.map((pageName) => (
   942	              <View
   943	                key={`carousel-indicator-track-${pageName}`}
   944	                style={styles.carouselIndicatorSegment}
   945	              />
   946	            ))}
   947	          </View>
   948	
   949	          <Animated.View
   950	            style={[
   951	              styles.carouselIndicatorActiveSegment,
   952	              {
   953	                width: indicatorSegmentWidth,
   954	                transform: [{ translateX: indicatorTranslateX }],
   955	              },
   956	            ]}
   957	          />
   958	        </Animated.View>
   959	      </Animated.View>
   960	
   961	    </View>
   962	  );
   963	
   964	  const centerShadow = (
   965	    <Animated.View
   966	      pointerEvents="none"
   967	      style={[
   968	        styles.headerCenterShadow,
   969	        {
   970	          opacity: centerShadowOpacity,
   971	          transform: [{ translateY: stickyOffset }],
   972	        },
   973	      ]}
   974	    >
   975	      {Platform.OS !== "web" && (
   976	        <>
   977	          {Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, index) => (
   978	            <View
   979	              key={`center-shadow-layer-${index + 1}`}
   980	              style={styles[`centerShadowLayer${index + 1}`]}
   981	            />
   982	          ))}
   983	        </>
   984	      )}
   985	    </Animated.View>
   986	  );
   987	
   988	  const hero = (
   989	    <View
   990	      style={styles.hero}
   991	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
   992	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
   993	    >
   994	      <Animated.Image
   995	        source={heroImageSource}
   996	        style={[
   997	          isMainHeroPage ? styles.heroImageWidthFit : styles.heroImage,
   998	          heroImageFrameStyle,
   999	          heroImageTransformStyle,
  1000	        ]}
  1001	        resizeMode={isMainHeroPage ? "contain" : "cover"}
  1002	      />
  1003	      {isMainHeroPage
  1004	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1005	            "cheeseboard-current",
  1006	            cheeseboardBaseTranslateX,
  1007	            cheeseboardBaseOpacity
  1008	          )
  1009	        : null}
  1010	      {isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
  1011	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1012	            "cheeseboard-outgoing",
  1013	            cheeseboardOutgoingTranslateX
  1014	          )
  1015	        : null}
  1016	      {shouldShowCheeseboardDragPreviews ? (
  1017	        <>
  1018	          {renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1019	            "cheeseboard-previous-preview",
  1020	            cheeseboardPreviousPreviewTranslateX
  1021	          )}
  1022	          {renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1023	            "cheeseboard-next-preview",
  1024	            cheeseboardNextPreviewTranslateX
  1025	          )}
  1026	        </>
  1027	      ) : null}
  1028	      {isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
  1029	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1030	            "cheeseboard-incoming",
  1031	            cheeseboardIncomingTranslateX,
  1032	            cheeseboardIncomingOpacity
  1033	          )
  1034	        : null}
  1035	      {isMainHeroPage ? (
  1036	        <Animated.View
  1037	          pointerEvents="none"
  1038	          style={[
  1039	            styles.mainHeroBackgroundFade,
  1040	            { opacity: mainHeroBackgroundFadeOpacity },
  1041	          ]}
  1042	        />
  1043	      ) : null}
  1044	      {!isMainHeroPage ? (
  1045	        <Animated.View
  1046	          pointerEvents="none"
  1047	          style={[
  1048	            styles.heroVerticalFadePanel,
  1049	            {
  1050	              transform: [{ translateY: heroVerticalFadeTranslateY }],
  1051	            },
  1052	          ]}
  1053	        >
  1054	          <LinearGradient
  1055	            colors={["rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)", heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor]}
  1056	            locations={[0, 1]}
  1057	            style={styles.heroVerticalFadeFeather}
  1058	          />
  1059	          <View
  1060	            style={[
  1061	              styles.heroVerticalFadeSolid,
  1062	              { backgroundColor: heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor },
  1063	            ]}
  1064	          />
  1065	        </Animated.View>
  1066	      ) : null}
  1067	    </View>
  1068	  );
  1069	
  1070	  const heroOnlyLayer = (
  1071	    <Animated.View
  1072	      style={[
  1073	        styles.header,
  1074	        {
  1075	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
  1076	        },
  1077	      ]}
  1078	    >
  1079	      {centerShadow}
  1080	      <View style={styles.heroOnlySpacer} />
  1081	      {Platform.OS === "ios" ? (
  1082	        <Animated.View
  1083	          style={{
  1084	            transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
  1085	          }}
  1086	        >
  1087	          {hero}
  1088	        </Animated.View>
  1089	      ) : (
  1090	        hero
  1091	      )}
  1092	    </Animated.View>
  1093	  );
  1094	
  1095	  if (showOnlyCarousel) return carousel;
  1096	  if (showOnlyHero) return heroOnlyLayer;
  1097	
  1098	  return (
  1099	    <Animated.View
  1100	      style={[
  1101	        styles.header,
  1102	        {
  1103	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
  1104	        },
  1105	      ]}
  1106	    >
  1107	      <Animated.View style={styles.orangeBar}>
  1108	        <Pressable style={styles.logoPressable} onPress={() => goToPage("home")}>
  1109	          <Text
  1110	            adjustsFontSizeToFit
  1111	            minimumFontScale={0.78}
  1112	            numberOfLines={1}
  1113	            style={styles.logoText}
  1114	          >
  1115	            Alla Vostra
  1116	          </Text>
  1117	        </Pressable>
  1118	
  1119	        {dimHeaderExceptShopButton ? (
  1120	          <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.orangeBarDimOverlay} />
  1121	        ) : null}
  1122	
  1123	        <Pressable
  1124	          style={[
  1125	            styles.shopButtonWrap,
  1126	            dimHeaderExceptShopButton && styles.shopButtonWrapSpotlight,
  1127	          ]}
  1128	          onPress={() => goToPage("shop")}
  1129	        >
  1130	          <ButtonShadowPlate style={styles.shopButtonShadowPlate} />
  1131	          <View style={styles.shopButton}>
  1132	            <Text style={styles.shopButtonText}>SHOP</Text>
  1133	          </View>
  1134	        </Pressable>
  1135	
  1136	        <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.orangeBarBottomHairline} />
  1137	      </Animated.View>
  1138	
  1139	      {showCarousel && showHero && centerShadow}
  1140	      {showCarousel && carousel}
  1141	      {showHero && hero}
  1142	    </Animated.View>
  1143	  );
  1144	}


============================================================
FILE: mob/components/PageDivider.js
============================================================

     1	import { Animated } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
     4	
     5	const topDividerFadeScrollDistance = 240;
     6	
     7	export default function PageDivider({
     8	  expandedSpacing = false,
     9	  fadeScrollDistance = topDividerFadeScrollDistance,
    10	  fadeWithScrollY = null,
    11	}) {
    12	  const fadeOpacity = fadeWithScrollY?.interpolate({
    13	    inputRange: [0, fadeScrollDistance],
    14	    outputRange: [1, 0],
    15	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    16	  });
    17	
    18	  return (
    19	    <Animated.View
    20	      style={[
    21	        sharedStyles.pageDivider,
    22	        expandedSpacing && sharedStyles.expandedPageDivider,
    23	        fadeOpacity && { opacity: fadeOpacity },
    24	      ]}
    25	    />
    26	  );
    27	}

     1	import { Image, Pressable, Text, View } from "react-native";

    30	          </Text>

    35	}

FILE: mob/components/ScreenFade.js

     1	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
     2	import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	export default function ScreenFade({ showTopFade = true, topOffset = 84 }) {
     5	  return (
     6	    <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.overlay}>
     7	      {showTopFade ? (
     8	        <LinearGradient
     9	          pointerEvents="none"
    10	          colors={[
    11	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.24)",
    12	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.14)",
    13	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.045)",
    14	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.008)",
    15	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.001)",
    16	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
    17	          ]}
    18	          locations={[0, 0.14, 0.32, 0.52, 0.72, 1]}
    19	          style={[styles.topFade, { top: topOffset }]}
    20	        />
    21	      ) : null}
    22	
    23	      <LinearGradient
    24	        pointerEvents="none"
    25	        colors={[
    26	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
    27	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.001)",
    28	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.008)",
    29	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.045)",
    30	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.14)",
    31	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.24)",
    32	        ]}
    33	        locations={[0, 0.28, 0.48, 0.68, 0.86, 1]}
    34	        style={styles.bottomFade}
    35	      />
    36	    </View>
    37	  );
    38	}
    39	
    40	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    41	  overlay: {
    42	    position: "absolute",
    43	    top: 0,
    44	    left: 0,
    45	    right: 0,
    46	    bottom: 0,
    47	    zIndex: 500000,
    48	    elevation: 500000,
    49	  },
    50	
    51	  topFade: {
    52	    position: "absolute",
    53	    left: 0,
    54	    right: 0,
    55	    height: 260,
    56	  },
    57	
    58	  bottomFade: {
    59	    position: "absolute",
    60	    left: 0,
    61	    right: 0,
    62	    bottom: 0,
    63	    height: 150,
    64	  },
    65	});

FILE: mob/utils/headerScrollContext.js
============================================================

     1	import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
     2	
     3	const HeaderScrollContext = createContext(null);
     4	
     5	export function HeaderScrollProvider({ children, scrollY }) {
     6	  return (
     7	    <HeaderScrollContext.Provider value={scrollY}>
     8	      {children}
     9	    </HeaderScrollContext.Provider>
    10	  );
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function useHeaderScrollY() {
    14	  return useContext(HeaderScrollContext);
    15	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/openPaymentLink.js
============================================================

     1	import { Alert, Linking, Platform } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	export async function openPaymentLink(url) {
     4	  if (!url) {
     5	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
     6	      window.alert("No payment link has been added for this product yet.");
     7	      return;
     8	    }
     9	
    10	    Alert.alert("Payment link missing", "No payment link has been added yet.");
    11	    return;
    12	  }
    13	
    14	  try {
    15	    const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
    16	
    17	    if (!canOpen) {
    18	      throw new Error("Cannot open URL");
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    await Linking.openURL(url);
    22	  } catch (error) {
    23	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
    24	      window.alert("Unable to open this payment link.");
    25	      return;
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    Alert.alert("Unable to open link", "Please try again later.");
    29	  }
    30	}

     1	module.exports = function (api) {
     2	  api.cache(true);
     3	
     4	  return {
     5	    presets: ["babel-preset-expo"],
     6	  };
     7	};


============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN APP SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The app snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime directories:
- mob/node_modules
- mob/.expo
- mob/.cache
- mob/.metro
- mob/.turbo
